002 Shenzhen Investment Limited 深圳控股有限公司 • Annual Report 年報 2021

Corporate Profile
公司簡介




     Shenzhen Investment Limited has been listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited since 1997.
     Shum Yip Group, being its controlling shareholder, is an integrated conglomerate directly managed by State-owned Assets
     Supervision and Administration Commission of Shenzhen Municipal. Positioned as an “Innovative Constructor of Industrial
      Cities, Wealthy Livelihood Operator”, the Company’s core businesses include property development, property investment
      and comprehensive urban operation services, and it also engages in innovative businesses such as high-tech agriculture
    and high-end manufacturing, endeavoring to transform and upgrade into a technology-based industry group focusing on the
                                development of urban complex and investment in technology industry.

    By intensifying its development in Shenzhen, focusing on the Guangdong-Hong Kong-Macao Greater Bay Area and planning
     for its development in the Yangtze River Delta and high-tier cities such as key provincial capital cities, the Group currently
    has a land reserve with gross floor area of approximately 6.74 million square meters in various cities of China, of which the
   quality land reserve with gross floor area of approximately 4.28 million square meters is located in the Greater Bay Area. The
   Company is committed to optimizing its land reserves structure, and concentrates its assets and business in the Greater Bay
                                            Area as well as high-tier cities in key regions.

     It has all along been the Company’s core strategic goal to promote the long term sustainable development of its business,
                        with an aim to bring a stable return and create value appreciation for its shareholders.

    深圳控股有限公司於1997年在香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，其控股股東深業集團是深圳市國資委直管的大型綜合性企業集
    團。公司按照“產城創新建設者、幸福民生運營商”的定位，以物業開發、物業投資及城市綜合運營服務為核心業務，同時涉足高
     科農業、高端製造等創新業務，致力於轉型升級成以城市綜合開發運營、科技產業的投資服務為主業的科技型產業集團。

    公司深耕深圳，聚焦粵港澳大灣區，佈局長三角區域及重點省會等高能級城市，目前本集團於國內多個城市合共擁有儲備建築面積
    約674萬平方米的土地儲備，其中位於大灣區的優質土地儲備的建築面積約428萬平方米。公司致力優化土地儲備的結構，將資產
                       和業務向大灣區和重點區域的高能級城市集中。

              公司一貫之核心策略目標，乃致力推動業務長期持續發展，以期為股東帶來整體穩定回報及締造價值增長。

new journey towards the second centenary goal. With its efforts to accelerate       百年奮鬥目標進軍新征程之際，我國加快構建新發展
the building of a new development pattern, comprehensively deepen the reform        格局，全面深化改革開放，堅持創新驅動發展，在推
and opening up and adhere to innovation-driving development, China has made         動高質量發展方面取得新成效，經濟發展和疫情防控
new achievements in promoting its high-quality development and maintained a         保持全球領先地位。當前百年變局與世紀疫情相互疊
global-leading position in economic development and pandemic prevention and         加，面對複雜嚴峻的環境，國內經濟仍然面臨需求收
control. In such a turbulent time interwoven with a once-in-a-century pandemic,     縮、供給衝擊、預期轉弱的三重壓力，不確定性及風
internally, facing the complex and severe environment, China’s economy is           險因素依然存在。
still under the “triple pressure”, namely shrinking demand, supply shocks and
weakening expectations, and the uncertainties and risk factors still exist.

Chairman’s Statement
主席報告




Real estate enterprises were confronted with unprecedented challenges in 2021.          房地產企業在2021年遭受前所未有的挑戰。上半年行
In the first half of the year, the real estate regulations were further tightened,      業監管加碼，「三道紅綫」和貸款集中度管理全面實
such as the nationwide implementation of the “Three Red Lines” policy and the           施，22城雙集中供地推行，不同能級城市表現有所區
loan concentration risk management system, as well as the implementation of             別，一綫城市韌性較強；二綫城市總體呈平穩態勢，
the “Double-Concentration Land Supply” policy in 22 cities. Against the backdrop,       沿海和內陸城市有所分化；三四綫城市回調加劇。下
the performance of cities at different tiers varied. The first-tier cities showed       半年，部份房企面臨流動性困境，市場整體信心受
strong resilience; the second-tier cities generally maintained stable, with certain     損，房產成交增長動能明顯轉弱，房企分化加劇，融
differences between coastal and inland cities; and in the third-tier and fourth-        資及償債壓力加大，地產及物業行業併購事件增多，
tier cities, market dropped further. In the second half of the year, as the liquidity   行業集中度進一步提升，優勝劣汰加速。受行業波動
concerns in some real estate enterprises damaged the overall market confidence,         影響，本集團持有的金融資產公允價值產生大幅度非
the growth momentum of real estate trading weakened significantly, the                  現金損失。
differentiation of real estate enterprises intensified, the pressure on financing and
debt repayment increased, and also more and more mergers and acquisitions
took place in the real estate and property industry, which made the industry more
concentrated and accelerated the survival of the fittest. Affected by fluctuations
in the industry, the fair value of financial assets held by the Group incurred a
significant non-cash loss.

In an increasingly changing and challenging environment, the Group forged               在加速變化、充滿挑戰的行業環境中，本集團堅持穩
ahead with courage and fortitude by adhering to the development concept of              中求進的發展理念，開拓進取，積極作為，資源拓展
prudent growth. It reached recent-year new highs in both resource expansion and         及地產銷售均創近年新高，戰略創新業務加速推進，
property sales. With the accelerated development of its strategically innovative        各主營業務板塊均取得了令人振奮的成績，在「十四
businesses and the satisfactory performance of all its principal business sectors,      五」開局之年邁出了堅實新步伐。
the Group has taken a solid new step in the opening year of the “14th Five-year
Plan”.

                                                                                                                                 主席報告




Satisfactory Contracted Sales and Substantial Increase in Booked                       合同銷售成績喜人，結轉收入大幅增長

effective mechanism, stabilize land prices, house prices and expectations, and

The Group’s assets are mainly denominated in Renminbi. 50% of the bank and            本集團的資產主要以人民幣為貨幣單位，銀行及其他

against HK$, the reporting currency. During the year, such fluctuations in the        及收益相對報告貨幣港幣的波動收益為2,765.4百萬港
asset and earnings denominated in Renminbi against HK$, the reporting currency,       元，計入其他綜合收益。本集團將密切監控並積極通

The Company is committed to maintaining high standard of corporate governance.      本公司致力恪守奉行高標準之企業管治。本公司董事

corporate governance is essential for effective management, a healthy corporate     健全之企業文化、成功之業務發展，以及提升股東價
culture, successful business growth and enhancing shareholders’ value. The          值至關重要。本公司已於整個2021年應用並遵守香港

Corporate Governance Code (“CG Code”) as set out in Appendix 14 to the Rules        錄十四所載之企業管治守則（「企業管治守則」）之所

(“Listing Rules”) throughout the year 2021.

Board of Directors                                                                  董事會
Responsibilities and Delegation                                                     職責及授權
Under the leadership of the Chairman, the Board is responsible for formulating      董事會在主席的領導下，負責制定本公司的整體策略
overall strategies and policies of the Company, supervising and evaluating          及政策，監督及評估公司的業務與財務表現；釐定適
business and financial performance of the Company, determining appropriate          用於公司情況的合適企業管治常規以及通過建立健全
corporate governance practices applicable to the Company’s circumstances and        的企業管治架構及制定適當的風險控制措施，確保其
ensuring operational efficiency and effectiveness by establishing a comprehensive   營運的效率及效益，致力提升長遠股東價值。
corporate governance framework with appropriate risk control measures and
aiming to enhance long-term shareholders’ value.

The Board has delegated certain functions to respective committees, including       董事會將若干職能授權予相關委員會，包括審核委員
Audit Committee, Nomination Committee and Remuneration Committee, the               會、提名委員會及薪酬委員會，該等委員會各自於
details of the responsibilities, membership and activities during the year of       年內之職責、成員身份及活動詳情載於下文「董事委
each of them are set out in the section headed “Board Committees” below.            員會」一節。該等董事委員會協助董事會履行職責，
These Board committees assist the Board to discharge its duties while the           而執行董事和高級管理人員則獲授權管理日常營運及
responsibilities of the day-to-day operations and business management are           業務。特定保留予董事會決定的事宜包括批准財務報
delegated to the executive directors and senior management. Matters specifically    表、股息政策、公司架構的重大變動、併購、主要
reserved to the Board include approval of financial statements, dividend policy,    投資、重大收購及出售資產、膺選董事、董事及高級
significant changes in corporate structure, mergers and acquisitions, major         管理層薪酬政策的變動、董事委員會職權範圍以及主

Remuneration Committee                                                               薪酬委員會
The Remuneration Committee comprises three independent non-executive                 薪酬委員會由三名獨立非執行董事組成。薪酬委員會
directors. It is chaired by Dr. WONG Yau Kar, David and other members are Mr.        由黃友嘉博士擔任主席，其他成員為李偉強先生及吳
LI Wai Keung and Mr. WU Wai Chung, Michael.                                          偉驄先生。

The Company adopted the model whereby the Remuneration Committee is                  本公司已採納由薪酬委員會負責根據其職權範圍就個
responsible for making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration               別執行董事及高級管理人員的薪酬待遇向董事會提出
packages of individual executive director and senior management according to its     建議的模式。薪酬委員會亦負責就非執行董事的薪酬
terms of reference. The Remuneration Committee is also responsible for making        事項向董事會提出建議及設立恰當及透明的程序以制
recommendation to the Board on the remuneration matters of non-executive             訂薪酬。薪酬委員會的職權範圍全文可於本公司網站
directors, the setting up of adequate and transparent procedures for setting such    及聯交所網站內查閱。
remuneration. The full terms of reference of the Remuneration Committee are
available on the Company’s website and the Stock Exchange’s website.

No director or any of his/her associates is allowed to determine their own           任何董事或其任何聯繫人士不得自行釐訂其本身的薪
remuneration. In making recommendation to the Board on the remuneration of           酬。在向董事會建議董事及高級管理人員的薪酬時，
directors and senior management, the Remuneration Committee shall consider           薪酬委員會須考慮同業薪酬水平、董事所付出的時間

of directors, employment conditions of other positions within the Group and          因素而釐定薪酬。

Risk Management and Internal Control                                                風險管理與內部監控
Responsibility                                                                      責任
The Board is responsible for the risk management and internal control systems       董事會須對風險管理及內部監控系統負責，有責任檢
and reviewing the effectiveness of such systems. Management is responsible for      討該等制度的有效性，而管理層則負責設計及執行內
designing and implementing an internal control system to manage the risks to the    部監控制度以最大限度的管理公司面臨的風險，達
Company to the maximum extent, in order to identify and manage these risks so       到識別和管理這些風險，從而可以掌握、降低、舒
as to understand, reduce, mitigate, divert or avoid these risks. The Board hereby   緩、轉移或規避這些風險。董事會謹在此闡釋該等系
explains that the systems are designed to manage, rather than eliminate, the risk   統旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且
of failure to meet business objectives, and only to provide reasonable, but not     只能就不會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕
absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss.                          對的保證。

Risk Management Process                                                                   風險管理程序
The Company adopts an integrated “bottom-up” and “top-down” risk review                   公司採用「由下而上」的方式配合「由上而下」的綜合
process, requiring the heads of functional departments and business units of the          風險檢討程序，要求公司職能部門及業務部門主管參
Company to participate in identifying operational risks and incorporate them into         與識別營運風險，納入各項業務及決策流程中，包括
various business and decision-making processes, including strategy formulation,           策略制定、業務發展、業務規劃、資金分配、投資
business development, business planning, capital allocation, investment                   決定、內部監控及日常營運等，以便於
decisions, internal controls and daily operations, to enable

(1)   comprehensive identification and prioritization of all significant risks within     (1)   全面識別公司內部所有的重大風險，並進行優
      the Company;                                                                              次排序；

(2)   reporting of significant risks to the appropriate management level;                 (2)   將重大風險上報至適當的管理層級別；

(3)   the management to effectively communicate about the risks.                          (3)   讓管理層就風險進行有效溝通。

Internal Control                                                                     內部監控
The internal control system of the Company is based on the principles under the      公司的內部監控系統是根據COSO (the Committee
COSO (the Committee of Sponsoring Organizations of the Treadway Commission).         of Sponsoring Organizations of the Treadway
The risk management department of the Company conducts an annual                     Commission)以下的原則所訂立，公司風險管理部每
assessment and review of the internal control and risk management systems.           年度對內部監控及風險管理系統進行認真評估與檢
The review covers five aspects including control environment, risk assessment,       討，檢討涵蓋監控環境、風險評估、監控活動、監
control activities, monitoring measures and information and communication, in        察措施及信息與溝通等五個方面，以此評估公司內部
order to assess whether the internal control and risk management systems of the      監控及風險管理系統是否穩健、妥善、有效及足夠。
Company is sound, appropriate, effective and sufficient. The risk management         風險管理部每年度形成公司內部監控及風險管理系統
department annually prepares an evaluation report on internal control and risk       評估報告。
management systems of the Company.

       business operation and governance, as well as regular review and                      以及對重要道德操守的定期檢討和進修培訓。
       refresher training on key ethical practices.

 •     The Company has compiled a compilation of human resource system,                •     公司制定了人力資源制度匯編並落實執行，

 We focused on auditing the valuation of Hengda Investment
 because the decrease in fair value of Hengda Investment is
 significant to the consolidated financial statements and the
 valuation of the Hengda Investment is subject to a high degree
 of estimation uncertainty, as the inherent risk in relation to the
 valuation is considered relatively higher due to the subjectivity of
 key assumptions used in the valuation and significant judgements
 involved in selecting the valuation methodology.
 我們專注對恒大投資估值的審計，是因為恒大投資的公允值減少對
 綜合財務報表而言意義重大，且由於估值所用關鍵假設的主觀性及
 於選擇估值方法時涉及重大判斷，與估值相關的固有風險被視為相
 對更高，故恒大投資估值存在高度估計不確定性。

 The valuation of Hengda Investment is, therefore, considered as a
 key audit matter.
 因此，恒大投資估值被視作關鍵審計事項。

                                                                   and procedures performed.

                                                                   到了所獲得的可用證據和所執行的程序的支持。

 We focused on auditing the assessment of NRV of PUD and PHS

 the estimation of the NRV is subject to a high degree of estimation
 uncertainty. The assessment of NRV of PUD and PHS is, therefore,

      5. The principal activities of the Group’s joint ventures and associates are       合營公司的主要業務載於附註18及19。
      described in Notes 18 and 19.

     （集團）有限公司), which is a private company incorporated in Hong Kong.                    （「深業（集團）」）。本公司之最終控股公司為

2     Summary of significant accounting policies                                     2   主要會計政策概要
      This note provides a list of the significant accounting policies adopted in        本附註載列編製該等綜合財務報表時採納之主要
      the preparation of these consolidated financial statements. These policies         會計政策。除另有指定外，該等政策已貫徹應
      have been consistently applied to all the year presented, unless otherwise         用於呈列的所有年度。
      stated.

      2.1 Basis of preparation                                                           2.1 編製基準
          (a) Going concern basis                                                            (a) 持續經營基準
               Due to the volatility of the property market in the PRC and the                   由於中國物業市場波動以及年內及年
               unfavourable impact on the presale performance during the                         結日後預售表現的不利影響，本公
               year and subsequent to the year end date, the directors of the                    司董事在評估本集團2021年12月31
               Company have given careful consideration to the future liquidity                  日後至少12 個月是否有充足財務資
               and performance of the Group and its available sources of                         源繼續持續經營時，已審慎考慮本
               financing in assessing whether the Group will have sufficient                     集團的未來流動資金及表現以及其可
               financial resources to continue as a going concern for at least                   用的融資資源。
               of 12 months from 31 December 2021.

    2.2 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                    2.2 合併及權益會計原則
        (a) Subsidiaries                                                                         (a) 附屬公司
             Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over                      附屬公司均為本集團擁有控制權的所
             which the Group has control. The Group controls an entity where                         有實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集
             the Group is exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from                        團承擔或擁有自其參與該實體產生之
             its involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect those                     可變回報之權利及有能力透過行使權
             returns through its power to direct the activities of the entity.                       力以影響其回報時，本集團即控制
             Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on which                              該實體。附屬公司自控制權轉移至
             control is transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated                            本集團之日起全面合併。附屬公司
             from the date that control ceases.                                                      自控制停止之日起解除合併。

               The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for                                本集團使用收購會計法對業務合併進
               business combinations by the group (refer to Note 2.3).                                    行會計處理（參閱附註2.3）。

               Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains                                  公司之間之交易、集團公司間交易
               on transactions between group companies are eliminated.                                    之結餘及未變現收益予以對銷。未
               Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the transaction                               變現虧損亦予以對銷，除非交易
               provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.                               有證據證明已轉讓資產出現減值。
               Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where                                附屬公司會計政策已按需要作出變
               necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted by                               動，以確保與本集團所採納之政策
               the group.                                                                                 貫徹一致。

               Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries                        附屬公司業績及權益中的非控股權益
               are shown separately in the consolidated statement of profit or                            分別於綜合損益表、全面收益表、
               loss, statement of comprehensive income, statement of changes                              權益變動表及財務狀況表中單獨呈
               in equity and statement of financial position respectively.                                列。

         (b)   Associates                                                                           (b)   聯營公司
               Associates are all entities over which the group has significant                           聯營公司指所有本集團對其擁有重大
               influence but not control or joint control. This is generally the                          影響力但無控制權或聯合控制權的實

               the equity method of accounting, after initially being recognised                          於初步按成本確認後採用權益會計法
               at cost.                                                                                   入賬。

      2.3 Business combinations                                                             2.3 業務合併
          The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all                       本集團採用收購會計法就所有業務合併入
          business combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments                       賬，而無論有否收購股權投資或其他資
          or other assets are acquired. The consideration transferred for the                   產。收購一間附屬公司的轉讓代價包括：
          acquisition of a subsidiary comprises the:

            •      fair values of the assets transferred                                      •   為所轉讓資產公允值

            •      liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business         •   對被收購業務前擁有人所產生的負債

            •      equity interests issued by the group                                       •   本集團所發行股權

            •      fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent           •   或有代價安排產生的任何資產或負債
                   consideration arrangement, and                                                 的公允值，及

            •      fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.          •   附屬公司任何先前存在股權的公允
                                                                                                  值。

             Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities          於業務合併時所收購的可識別資產及所承
             assumed in a business combination are, with limited exceptions,                  擔的負債及或有負債，初始按收購當日的
             measured initially at their fair values at the acquisition date.                 公允值計量，惟少數例外情況除外。

             Concentration test as prescribed by IFRS 3 “Business Combination”                本集團已應用國際財務報告準則第3號「業
             has been applied by the Group to consider whether the set of                     務合併」所規定的集中度測試，以考慮所
             activities and assets of subsidiaries acquired are individually                  收購附屬公司的一系列活動及資產是否為
             businesses. If the fair value of total net assets acquired substantially         個別業務。倘所收購資產淨值總額的公
             equals to the fair value of an identifiable asset acquired, the                  允值與已收購可識別資產的公允值大致相
             transaction would be accounted for as asset acquisition instead of               若，則交易將作為資產收購而非業務收購
             business acquisition. The consideration is then directly allocated to            入賬。屆時會將代價直接分配至收購的個
             the individual identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed.             別可識別資產及承擔的負債。

             The group recognises any non-controlling interest in the acquired                本集團根據個別收購交易按公允值或非控

             entity’s net identifiable assets.                                                益。

             Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                              收購相關成本於產生時支銷。

          Property, plant and equipment are stated at historical cost less                           物業、廠房及設備均以歷史成本扣除折舊

             Subsequent costs are included in the asset’s carrying amount or                       後續成本只有在很可能為本集團帶來與該
             recognised as a separate asset, as appropriate, only when it is                       項目有關的未來經濟利益，而該項目的成
             probable that future economic benefits associated with the item will                  本能可靠計量時，才包括在資產的賬面值
             flow to the Group and the cost of the item can be measured reliably.                  或確認為一項單獨資產（如適用）。已更
             The carrying amount of the replaced part is derecognised. All other                   換部份的賬面值已被終止確認。所有其他
             repairs and maintenance are charged to profit or loss during the                      維修及保養開支乃於其產生之財政期間內
             financial period in which they are incurred.                                          自損益扣除。

         be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for the amount by                      資產的賬面值超出其可收回金額的差額確
         which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount.                       認。可收回金額為資產的公允值減出售成
         The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value                           本及使用價值兩者中的較高者。於評估減
         less costs to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing                      值時，資產按獨立可識別現金流量（現金

         are separately identifiable cash flows (cash-generating units). Non-                    商譽外已蒙受減值的非金融資產於各報告
         financial assets other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are                    期末均就減值是否可以撥回進行檢討。
         reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at each of each

    2.13 Impairment of financial assets                                                      2.13 金融資產減值
         The Group recognises a loss allowance for expected credit losses                         本集團就根據香港財務報告準則第9 號須
         (“ECL”) on financial assets which are subject to impairment under                        予減值的金融資產（包括貿易及其他應收
         HKFRS 9 (including trade and other receivables, loans to joint                           款、給予合營公司及聯營公司之貸款及現
         ventures and associates and cash and more than that).                                    金及其他）的預期信用損失（「預期信用損
                                                                                                  失」）確認損失撥備。

         The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect                             預期信用損失金額於各報告日更新，反映
         changes in credit risk since initial recognition. Lifetime ECL represents                  自初始確認起的信用風險變動。存續期預
         the ECL that will result from all possible default events over the                         期信用損失指相關工具的預期使用期內所
         expected life of the relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL                        有可能的違約事件產生之預期信用損失。
         (“12m ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to                     相反，12個月預期信用損失（「12個月預
         result from default events that are possible within 12 months after the                    期信用損失」）將預期於報告日期後12 個
         reporting date. Assessment are done based on the Group’s historical                        月內可能發生的違約事件預期導致之存續
         credit loss experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the                      期預期信用損失部份。評估乃根據本集團
         debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment of both the                         的歷史信用損失經驗進行，並根據債務人
         current conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast of                        特有的因素、一般經濟狀況以及對報告日
         future conditions.                                                                         期當前狀況的評估以及對未來狀況的預測
                                                                                                    作出調整。

         The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade receivables. To                         本集團始終就應收賬款確認整個存續期預
         measure ECL, trade receivables have been grouped based on shared                           期信用損失。為計量預期信用損失，應收
         credit risk characteristics and the days past due. The ECL on these                        賬款已按共有之信貸風險特徵及逾期天數
         assets are assessed using simplified expected credit loss model.                           分組。該等資產之預期信用損失乃使用簡
                                                                                                    化預期信用損失模式評估。

         For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss allowance                           對於所有其他工具，本集團計量的損失撥
         equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been a significant increase                        備相當於12個月預期信用損失，除非自初
         in credit risk since initial recognition, the Group recognizes lifetime                    始確認起信用風險出現顯著增長，則本集
         ECL. The assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                           團確認存續期間預期信用損失。是否應確
         is based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk of a default                   認存續期預期信用損失的評估乃基於自初
         occurring since initial recognition.                                                       始確認以來發生違約之可能性或風險的顯
                                                                                                    著增加。

    2.14 Offsetting financial instruments                                                    2.14 抵銷金融工具
         Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount reported                  若具法律強制執行權利以抵銷已確認款項
         in the consolidated statement of financial position when there is                        及有意按淨額基準結算，或同時變現資產
         a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts and                         及清償負債，則金融資產及負債可予以抵
         there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise the asset and                  銷，而有關淨額則於綜合財務狀況表內呈
         settle the liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must                  報。法定可執行權利必須不得依賴未來事
         not be contingent on future events and must be enforceable in the                        件而定，而在一般業務過程中以及倘本公
         normal course of business and in the event of default, insolvency or                     司或對手方一旦出現違約、無償債能力或
         bankruptcy of the company or the counterparty.                                           破產時，這也必須具有約束力。

         trade and other receivables is expected in one year or less (or in the                   其他應收款預期於一年或更短時間內（或

    2.19 Contract assets and contract liabilities                                            2.19 合約資產及合約負債
         Upon entering into a contract with a buyer, the Group obtains rights                     在與買家簽訂合約時，本集團有權獲得來
         to receive consideration from the buyer and assumes performance                          自買家支付的合約價款，與此同時承擔將
         obligations to transfer goods or provide services to the buyer. The                      貨物或服務轉移給買家的履約義務。這些
         combination of those rights and performance obligations gives rise to                    權利和履行義務的結合導致了淨資產或淨
         a net asset or a net liability depending on the relationship between                     負債的產生，這取決於剩餘權利和履行義
         the remaining rights and the performance obligations. The contract                       務之間的關係。合約計量的剩餘收款權
         is an asset and recognised as contract assets if the measure of the                      超過了剩餘履約義務的部份確認為合約資
         remaining rights exceeds the measure of the remaining performance                        產。反之，剩餘履約義務超過剩餘收款權
         obligations. Conversely, the contract is a liability and recognised                      的部份確認為合約負債。
         as contract liabilities if the measure of the remaining performance
         obligations exceeds the measure of the remaining rights.

         The Group recognises the incremental costs of obtaining a contract                         本集團為獲得與買家簽訂的合約而產生的
         with a buyer as contract acquisition cost within contract assets if the                    增量成本預計可收回時，則將其確認為合
         Group expects to recover those costs.                                                      約資產中的合約取得成本。

    2.20 Cash and bank balances                                                              2.20 現金及銀行結餘
         For the purpose of presentation in the statement of cash flows, cash                     就呈列現金流量表而言，現金及等同現金
         and cash equivalents includes cash on hand, deposits held at call with                   項目包括手頭現金、存放於金融機構的活
         financial institutions, other short-term, highly liquid investments with                 期存款、其他短期高流通投資（原本到期
         original maturities of three months or less that are readily convertible                 日不超過三個月，並可即時轉換為已知數
         to known amounts of cash and which are subject to an insignificant                       額的現金，惟須受非重大價值變動風險所
         risk of changes in value, and bank overdrafts. Bank overdrafts are                       限）及銀行透支。於資產負債表中，銀行
         shown within borrowings in current liabilities in the balance sheet.                     透支列入流動負債的借款內。

      2.22 Government grants                                                                 2.22 政府補助
           Grants from the government are recognised at their fair value where                    當能合理確定將收到之政府補助，而本集
           there is a reasonable assurance that the grant will be received and                    團將遵守所有附帶條件時，補助按其公允
           the Group will comply with all attached conditions.                                    值確認。

             Government grants relating to costs are deferred and recognised in                與成本有關之政府補助遞延處理，並於利
             the profit or loss over the period necessary to match them with the               用該等資助用以補償該等成本的期間在損
             costs that they are intended to compensate.                                       益內確認入賬。

             Government grants relating to the purchase of property, plant and                 有關購買物業、廠房及設備的政府補助則
             equipment are included in non-current liabilities as deferred income              在非流動負債中列作遞延收入，並在損益
             and are credited to profit or loss on a straight-line basis over the              內以直線法於相關資產預計年期攤銷。
             expected lives of the related assets.

      2.23 Trade and other payables                                                          2.23 貿易及其他應付款
           Trade payables are obligations to pay for goods or services that have                  應付賬款為於日常業務過程中從供應商購
           been acquired in the ordinary course of business from suppliers.                       入貨品或服務之付款責任。如果付款於一
           Trade payables are classified as current liabilities if payment is                     年內或以內（或倘時間較長，則於正常之
           due within one year or less (or in the normal operating cycle of                       業務運營週期內）到期，則將應付賬款分
           the business if longer). If not, they are presented as non-current                     類為流動負債。否則，呈列為非流動負
           liabilities.                                                                           債。

             Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair value and               貿易及其他應付款初始按公允值確認，其
             subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective interest              後以實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。
             method.

    2.24 Borrowings                                                                          2.24 借貸
         Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction                    借貸初始按公允值扣除所產生的交易成本
         costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised                        確認。借貸其後按攤銷成本計量，如扣除
         cost; any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction costs)                     交易成本之後的所得收益與贖回價值出現
         and the redemption value is recognised in profit or loss over the                        差額，則於借貸期內以實際利率法在損益
         period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.                            表中確認。

         Borrowings are removed from the balance sheet when the obligation                          當合約中規定之責任解除、取消或屆滿
         specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled or expired. The                         時，借貸自資產負債表中剔除。已銷除或
         difference between the carrying amount of a financial liability that                       轉讓予另一方之金融負債之賬面值與已支
         has been extinguished or transferred to another party and the                              付代價（包括任何已轉讓之非現金資產或
         consideration paid, including any non-cash assets transferred or                           所承擔之負債）之間之差額，在損益中確
         liabilities assumed, is recognised in profit or loss as finance costs.                     認為財務成本。

         Where the terms of a financial liability are renegotiated and the entity                   倘重新議定一項金融負債的條款，而實體
         issues equity instruments to a creditor to extinguish all or part of the                   向債權人發行股本工具以消除所有或部份
         liability (debt for equity swap), a gain or loss is recognised in profit                   負債（債務或權益互換），則相關盈虧於
         or loss, which is measured as the difference between the carrying                          損益內確認，並按該金融負債的賬面值與
         amount of the financial liability and the fair value of the equity                         發行的權益工具的公允值之差額計量。
         instruments issued.

         Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group has                      除非本集團有權無條件將債務清償遞延至
         an unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for at least                   報告期後至少12個月，否則借貸將被分類
         12 months after the reporting period.                                                      為流動負債。

    2.25 Borrowing costs                                                                     2.25 借貸成本
         General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable to the                        直接歸屬於收購、興建或生產合資格資產
         acquisition, construction or production of qualifying assets, which are                 （須經較長籌備期方可作擬定用途或銷售
         assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready                   的資產）的一般及特定借款成本會加入該
         for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those assets,                   等資產的成本中，直至資產大致上備妥可
         until such time as the assets are substantially ready for their intended                 作擬定用途或銷售為止。
         use or sale.

         Investment income earned on the temporary investment of specific                           在特定借款撥作合資格資產的支出前暫時
         borrowings pending their expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted                      用作投資所賺取的投資收入會自可撥充資
         from the borrowing costs eligible for capitalisation.                                      本的借款成本中扣除。

         All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the period                   所有其他借貸成本於其產生期間在損益確
         in which they are incurred.                                                                認。

      2.26 Current and deferred income tax                                                  2.26 當期及遞延所得稅
           The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on                 期內所得稅費用或抵免為當期應課稅收入
           the current period’s taxable income based on the applicable income                    按各司法權區適用所得稅率之應付稅項，
           tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax                    經暫時差異及未使用稅項虧損產生的遞延
           assets and liabilities attributable to temporary differences and to                   稅項資產及負債變動調整。
           unused tax losses.

                   The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of                      即期所得稅開支乃根據本公司及其附

                   income. Management periodically evaluates positions taken in                     層就適用稅務法規詮釋所規限的情況
                   tax returns with respect to situations in which applicable tax                   定期評估報稅表的狀況，並權衡稅
                   regulation is subject to interpretation and considers whether it                 務機關是否可能接受不確定稅務處
                   is probable that a taxation authority will accept an uncertain tax               理。本集團基於最大可能金額或預
                   treatment. The Group measures its tax balances either based                      期價值計量稅項結餘，取決於哪種
                   on the most likely amount or the expected value, depending on                    方法提供不確定性解決方法的更佳預
                   which method provides a better prediction of the resolution of                   測。
                   the uncertainty.

                   Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability                     就資產和負債的稅基與資產和負債在
                   method, on temporary differences arising between the tax                         綜合財務報表的賬面值的差額而產生
                   bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts                       的暫時性差異利用負債法撥備遞延所
                   in the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred                      得稅。然而，倘遞延稅項負債來自

                   liability in a transaction other than a business combination that                而該等資產或負債在交易時不影響
                   at the time of the transaction affects neither accounting nor                    會計或應課稅損益及不會導致等同
                   taxable profit or loss and does not give rise to equal taxable                   應課稅及可扣減差異，則同樣不作
                   and deductible temporary differences. Deferred income tax is                     記賬。遞延所得稅以報告期末前頒
                   determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or                  佈或實質上頒佈的稅率（及法例）釐
                   substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period and are                 定，且該等稅率預期將於相關遞延
                   expected to apply when the related deferred income tax asset is                  所得稅資產變現或遞延所得稅負債償

    2.28 Share-based payments                                                                2.28 以股份為基礎之付款
         The Group operates a number of equity-settled, share-based                               本集團設有多項以權益結算以股份為基礎

         the same class of obligations may be small.

         and the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision                   時間流逝引起之撥備增加確認為利息開
         due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.                                 支。

         Income is classified by the Group as revenue when it arises from the                    當本集團於日常業務過程中提供服務或他

         Group’s assets under leases in the ordinary course of the Group’s                       品收入時，本集團將收入分類為收入。
         business.

         Revenue is recognised when control over a product or service is                           當產品或服務之控制權轉移至客戶或承租
         transferred to the customer, or the lessee has the right to use the                       人有權使用資產時，按本集團預期將享有
         asset, at the amount of promised consideration to which the Group is                      的承諾代價金額確認收入，惟不包括代第
         expected to be entitled, excluding those amounts collected on behalf                      三方收取的款項。收入不包括增值稅或其
         of third parties. Revenue excludes value added tax or other sales                         他銷售稅，並經扣減任何貿易折扣後計
         taxes and is after deduction of any trade discounts.                                      算。

         Where the contract contains a financing component which provides                          倘合約載有向客戶提供超過12個月的重大
         a significant financing benefit to the customer for more than 12                          融資利益的融資組成部份，則收入按應收
         months, revenue is measured at the present value of the amount                            金額的現值計量，並使用反映於與有關客
         receivable, discounted using the discount rate that would be reflected                    戶的個別融資交易的折現率貼現，且利息
         in a separate financing transaction with the customer, and interest                       收入根據實際利率法分開累計。倘合約載
         income is accrued separately under the effective interest method.                         有向本集團提供重大融資利益的融資組成
         Where the contract contains a financing component which provides a                        部份，則根據該合約確認的收入包括根據
         significant financing benefit to the Group, revenue recognised under                      實際利率法合約責任附有的利息開支。
         that contract includes the interest expense accreted on the contract
         liability under the effective interest method.

    risk management programme focuses on the unpredictability of financial                    本集團之整體風險管理計劃針對金融市場不可預

    financial performance. The property industry is highly sensitive to the                   潛在負面影響。房地產業對中國的經濟環境高
    economic environment in the PRC, which will affect the volumes of                         度敏感，經濟環境影響物業交易量及售價。本

             The definition of credit-impaired assets                                                信貸減值資產的定義
             Under HKFRS 9, in order to determine whether credit impairment                          根據香港財務報告準則第9號，為釐
             occurs, the defined standards adopted by the Group are                                  定是否出現信貸減值，本集團於考
             consistent with the internal credit risk management objectives                          慮定性及定量標準所採納界定準則與
             for relevant financial assets, while considering quantitative and                       相關金融資產的內部信貸風險管理目
             qualitative indicators. When the Group assesses whether the                             標一致。本集團評估債務人是否出
             debtor has credit impairment, the following factors are mainly                          現信貸減值時，主要考慮下列因素：
             considered:

               •     The debt has overdue for more than 90 days after the                                 •      債務於合約付款日期後欠款逾
                     contract payment date.                                                                      期超過90日。
               •     The lender gives the debtor concessions for economic                                 •      貸款人因債務人遇上財務困難
                     or contractual reasons due to the debtor’s financial                                        而基於經濟或合約理由向債務
                     difficulties, where such concessions are normally reluctant                                 人授出貸款人於一般情況下不
                     to be made by the lender.                                                                   願意授出的寬免。
               •     The debtor has significant financial difficulties.                                   •      債務人遇上重大財務困難。
               •     The debtor is likely to go bankrupt or needs other financial                         •      債務人很可能破產或需要其他
                     restructuring.                                                                              財務重組。

               the joint effects of multiple events, and may not be caused by                             共同影響，不一定因個別可識別事
               separately identifiable events.                                                            件所致。

               the ability of the debtors to settle the receivables.                                      濟因素的當前和前瞻性資料。

               regularly evaluates the results.                                                           最佳估計，並定期評估有關結果。

    3.2 Capital management                                                                  3.2 資本管理
        The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the                       本集團管理資本目標為維持本集團持續經
        Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide                      營能力，以為股東提供回報為其他利益相
        returns for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders and to                     關方創造利益，並保持最佳資本結構以減
        maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.                    低資金成本。

         In order to optimise the capital structure, the Group may adjust the                     為優化資本架構，本集團可能會調整已向
         amount of dividends paid to shareholders, buy back of shares, issue                      股東支付的股息金額、購回股份、發行新
         new shares or sell assets.                                                               股或出售資產。

4   Critical accounting estimates and judgements                                     4     關鍵會計估計及判斷
    Estimates and judgements used in preparing the consolidated financial                  評估在編製綜合財務報表時使用的估計及判斷乃
    statements are evaluated and based on historical experience and other                  以過往經驗及其他因素（包括在當時情況下被視
    factors, including expectations of future events that are believed to be               為合理的未來事件的預期）為依據。本集團對未
    reasonable under the circumstances. The Group makes estimates and                      來作出估計及假設。根據定義，所得的會計估
    assumptions concerning the future. The resulting accounting estimates will,            計極少與相關的實際結果相同。下文討論可能
    by definition, seldom equal the related actual results. The estimates and              對下個財政年度的資產和負債賬面值有重大影響

    (e) Land appreciation taxes                                                              (e) 土地增值稅

        proceeds of sales of properties less deductible expenditures including                   地增值部份即出售物業所得款項減包括土

                                                                                                 內之可扣減開支。

          The subsidiaries of the Group engaging in property development                            本集團附屬公司於中國從事物業開發業務

          have been included in the income tax expenses. However, the                               支內。然而，中國多個城市對該等稅項之

          and the Group has not finalised its land appreciation tax returns with                    稅務機關落實土地增值稅報稅表。因此，
          various tax authorities for certain projects. Accordingly, judgement                      釐定土地增值及其有關應付稅項金額時須
          is required in determining the amount of land appreciation and its                        作出判斷。本集團根據管理層之最佳估計
          related taxes payable. The Group recognises these liabilities based                       確認該等負債。倘有關事宜之最終稅款有

29 Share option scheme                                                               29 購股權計劃
   The Company operated a share option scheme (the “Scheme”) which was                     本公司設有一項購股權計劃（「計劃」）於2012
   approved and adopted on 22 June 2012 for the purpose of providing                       年6 月22 日批准及採納，以對本集團業務成
   incentives and rewards to directors and employees of the Group who                      功作出貢獻之本集團董事及僱員提供鼓勵及獎
   contribute to the success of the Group’s operations. The Scheme became                  勵。計劃於2012年6月22日生效，而除非被取
   effective on 22 June 2012 and, unless otherwise cancelled or amended,                   消或經修訂，否則該計劃將於當日起計十年內
   shall be valid and effective for a period of 10 year from that date, after              有效。有效期屆滿後，則不會再授出任何購股
   which period no further options will be issued but in all other respects the            權，但在所有其他方面，計劃條文仍具十足效
   provisions of the Scheme shall remain in full force and effect.                         力及作用。

32 Contract liabilities (continued)                                                     32 合約負債（續）
    Typical payment terms which impact on the amount of contract liabilities of               對已確認物業開發合約負債金額構成影響之一般
    property development recognised are as follows:                                           支付條款如下：

    The Group receives certain amount of deposit from customers when they                     本集團於客戶簽署買賣協議時自彼等收取若干訂
    sign the sale and purchase agreements. These deposits are recognised as                   金。該等訂金確認為合約負債，直至有關物業
    contract liabilities until the relevant properties are completed and ready for            落成並可供轉讓予客戶為止。
    hand-over to the customers.

    The Group may require the customers to pay off the remaining balance of                   本集團可要求客戶於建造工程仍在進行之協定時
    the consideration within an agreed timeframe while constructions are still                間內而非於有關物業落成時償還剩餘代價。有
    ongoing, rather than on the completion of the relevant properties. Such                   關提前付款計劃會導致於整個剩餘物業建造期間
    advance payment schemes result in contract liabilities being recognised                   按全額合約價格確認合約負債。此外，本集團
    throughout the remaining property construction period for the full amount                 用以反映於付款日期與轉讓日期期間自客戶獲得
    of the contract price. In addition, the contract liabilities will be increased            的任何重大融資利益影響之應計利息開支金額將
    by the amount of interest expense being accrued by the Group to reflect                   會增加合約負債。由於此應計費用會增加建造
    the effect of any significant financing benefit obtained from the customers               期間合約負債之金額，故其會增加落成物業之
    during the period between the payment and hand-over date. As this                         控制權轉讓予客戶時已確認收益之金額。
    accrual increases the amount of the contract liabilities during the period of
    construction, it therefore increases the amount of revenue recognised when
    control of the completed property is transferred to the customer.
